Step 2.5 substrate-readiness: ARC-050 (play-as-SD-012-curriculum) tests whether synthetic z_goal seeding during a play episode bypasses the drive_level requirement to bootstrap E3 goal-pursuit competence. Core mechanism has NO substrate -- ree_core has no is_play/play_frame/synthetic-z_goal-target injection path; the only z_goal seeding path is GoalState.update() (ree-v3/ree_core/goal.py:639-712), gated by drive_level (effective_benefit past benefit_threshold=0.1); z_goal_inject (MECH-188) is only an action-selection norm-floor, not a structured synthetic target. ARC-050 depends_on ARC-049 (play_frame_tag) + MECH-194 (synthetic signal substitution), both status=candidate/substrate_conditional + UNIMPLEMENTED (verified firsthand 2026-06-22). A commitment-free competence DV (goal_proximity, z_goal_norm) exists, but there is no intervention to apply without the substrate. NO script written, NO queue entry. EVB-0261 marked blocked_substrate with durable gating_reason+release_condition; build_experiment_indexes.py re-run (status propagated to full+index). Route: /implement-substrate on ARC-049+MECH-194 (V3 degenerate play-frame form). Sibling precedent: ARC-046 -> blocked_substrate 2026-05-29 (IGW-027) on upstream z_goal-collapse. NOTE: duplicate staging IGW-20260619-185 (same claim ARC-050, never launched, no artifacts) is superseded by this disposition.
